### Post #14: 2025-01-21 18:24 — `$RCAT` `[Dd]`

**Bullish on $ONDS**

Drone stocks have boomed, stagnated and fallen off from late 2024 growth due to the hype on the east coast. $RCAT has fallen nearly 50% since it hit all time highs while $ONDS has fallen around 40. 

These are great entry points. 

ONDS is seeing aggresive support around the 2 dollar mark and they are making a lot of changes behind the scenes for growth plays including anonymous funding and tactical board editions. Today their CEO Eric Brock was voted co-chair of the commercial drone alliance which has ties to the White House and other government and commercial entities. One of their board members was also recently appointed as ambassador to the Netherlands by Trump. 

Why does this matter?

Trump is pushing out immigration and tightening up boarders- specifically in the south. he’s declared a national emergency and is sending troops to the wall and designated cartels as terrorist groups. These acts require a lot of manpower and watchful eyes that isn’t logistically feasible unless we leverage technology to assist in surveillance and security. 

This is where drones come in - specifically $ONDS who is already working with defense contractors and commercial entities and recently secured its BVLOS waiver from the FAA. Instead of having a sentry posted every mile (649 of them btw). You could have a small team of drone operators managing individual swarms for a fraction of the cost. 

This could also extend and become the norm for domestic police forces and private security. 

I don’t think the recent catalysts have been priced in and I think we could be heading for a big 2025 with $ONDS due to all of the signs of growth we are seeing across their business. 

I doubled down today on shares and have left my options open, planning on adding leaps when we get longer exps as volume picks up. 

I’d love to know your thoughts on $ONDS and what potential scenarios you see playing out!

### Post #18: 2025-07-09 06:53 `[Discuss]`

**$ONDS may dump tomorrow and that’s fine.**

I got a lot of flak on my near term bearish $ONDS outlook due to today’s blog post from CEO Eric Brock. 

To be clear going into investors day July 9th I was originally bullish and thought we might see $3 on good guidance. I don’t think that is the case - yet. In this fledgling industry first to the the pie wins and Ondas is full steam ahead with growth at the center of its focus. Their output of product for multiple multi-million dollar purchase orders doesn’t just come out of thin air, especially for a small cap like this. They need infrastructure, partnerships, and capital. These things take time but are nescessary for winners and I think we found our gift horse if you give it time to run. 

 This piece of the blog post is what caught my eye and led to my short term bearish sentiment. 

“Venture capital has played a critical role in nurturing this ecosystem, backing the R&D and early platform development that brought us here. But the next leg of growth, service delivery at scale, requires a different kind of capital. It requires growth capital:
 
Capital that comes with lower cost, longer horizons, and a focus on scaling operations, not just innovating in a lab.
Capital that is patient, strategic, and grounded in public market discipline.”

Now for my reasoning:

We already had news releases on their issue of common stock last month - there is no need to reiterate on the official blog. And the timing… Why would the CEO post something like this right before investors day and not during? Simple - he is trying to to blunt negative sentiment on a new dilution announcement tomorrow for a common stock offering to fuel rampant growth. If this happens the stock may sell off on the retail side while hedge funds load up for cheap. 

Why should you care?

This company is special. Their execution on future guidance, especially recently, has been near flawless. We are seeing catalysts of new strategic partnerships on the finance, marketing, and production side of things almost weekly. If the stock does sink tomorrow on news of dilution buy and HOLD. THIS. IS. A. WINNER

I want to know your thoughts and also the reason why you think so not just “You’re wrong” or “You’re right” let’s have a discussion. 

$ONDS to the moon baby!! 🚀🚀🚀

### Post #19: 2025-07-11 05:55 — `$RCAT, $ONDS, $UMAC` `[Gain]` `[Gain]`

**Red tape is gone for $ONDS and other drone stocks.**

$ONDS has been on a roll for the last few months and we’re just getting started. As of today Sec Def removed the “red tape” and opened the floodgates of domestic drone production and acquisition. US colonels and captains now have the power to independently procure and test drones as long as they fall under national security criteria ($ONDS and $RCAT have these). 

Now what you need to understand about drones is that one size does not fit all, each has their niche. 

1. $RCATs Black Widow drone specializes in squad recon and won the short range reconnaissance program contract which was judged by active duty soldiers (think about who can make purchase orders now).

2. $ONDS has the Iron Drone raider - a counter UAS system meant to neutralize enemy drones. They also have the Optimus system - that specializes in autonomous security and data collection - think border security and first responder support (imagine if we had this infrastructure in place for the horrible flood in Texas - people in danger could be identified quicker by autonomous systems and saved by first responders who aren’t wasting precious minutes trying to find them.)

3. $UMAC also needs to be mentioned. This company manufactures drone components that follow the Blue UAS framework and are NDAA compliant meaning they are approved for use by the department of defense. They are also about to open a new 17,000 square foot drone motor facility to fulfill this fresh surge of domestic production. Did I mention Donald Trump Jr. sits on their advisory board?

The writing is on the walls… or the drones? What are you guys going to do about it?

Stay tuned.

Theory time —

We all know $ONDS is killing it and is on a 🚀🚀🚀 to the moon with 60% 90% and 333% 1 week, 1 month, and 3 month gains respectively. All rockets run out of fuel eventually and due to the huge short term gains I’ve been bearish on earnings with 3 consecutive misses.

Earlier this week Sec of Transportation Duffy held a “Unleashing American Drone Dominance” event. We had small companies Walmart (800 billion market cap) and DoorDash (100 billion market cap) on the right and an AMERICAN FUCKING ROBOTICS DRONE ($ONDS Subsidiary) on the left.

See the below article excerpt.

 “Bedford inherits an FAA under pressure to overhaul outdated air traffic control systems, some dating back to the 1960s, which have caused outages and near-misses. These aging systems struggle to accommodate the growing presence of drones in the National Airspace System (NAS). Bedford has pledged to prioritize a $12.5 billion modernization plan, described by Transportation Secretary Sean Duffy as a “down payment” for upgrades. Ondas isn’t just a drone company, they’re also a tech company specializing in digitizing private wireless networks.

**The Power of Cash Secured Puts in a Bull Run**

Are you tired of losing your money buying lotto calls and having them expire worthless? Then do I have a deal for you!

Cash secured puts are a great way to generate income on your cash while also having the opportunity to pick up stocks you want at a great price. This strategy can be especially lucrative (but also risky) on micro cap stocks due to high IV which is what fuels the premium payment you receive (more uncertainty = more risk = more reward. 

Quick example: Let’s say our king $ONDS is trading for $3.50. You could sell a 1 week CSP for 3.25. You are paid a premium for the obligation to purchase 100 shares of $ONDS per contract at 3.25 regardless of what the current price is. If the stock is above the breakeven you will most likely not be assigned, you keep the premium, and your collateral is returned to you. Congrats you got free money. This is a great way to generate income on a stock you want while having the opportunity to get in at a good price on a dip. 

You could also be a degen like me and sell out of the money puts. For example selling a a 4 dollar strike put while $ONDS  is 3.50. The premium is much higher but also the risk of being assigned. If the stock dropped to 2.50 you’d have to purchase it at 4. The reason I like this strategy in the current climate is because we are seeing such rapid price movement that I’ve been able to close on my puts for a credit before exp to load up on more shares. Im also not worried about assignment because I can roll the put to a later EXP or bite the loss and hold the shares anyway because I have conviction this is a multibagger. I haven’t lost a CSP this month. 

Keep in mind this is 6k in a month on my 15k hood port. You do the math. 


*Link: https://afterhour.com/Drone_Daddy/Wmm/the-power-of-cash-secured-puts*

### Post #83: 2025-09-15 20:09 — `$LPTH, $ONDS, $SPAI, $UMAC, $AMPX` `[Dd]`

**$ONDS and $UMAC Strategic investment in $LPTH**

Ondas and Unusual machines agree to 8 million dollar strategic investment In lightpath for their optic and imaging systems. 

This is important because $LPTH tech does not require germanium for their infared cameras. 

What is germanium?

It’s an element used to make lenses for infared cameras and imaging tech. It’s also mainly exported from China and isn’t easy to get. 

$LPTH offers an alternative solution called Black Diamond glass (made in the U.S.) that is cheaper, smaller, and lighter giving more options for additional payloads, tech, and battery solutions. 

$UMAC and $ONDS names on acquisitions and investments are becoming more and more common. I am unbelievably bullish on these companies as well as ones like $SPAI ,$LPTH , and $AMPX as their use cases will expand with larger and larger demands over the next 5 years.

### Post #97: 2025-09-22 20:12 — `$ONDS` `[Dd]`

**Hopping on the $ONDS train has more than one meaning…**

They are the perfect business positioned to fill multiple industry needs under the department of transportation not just CUAS and Drones.

Ondas’ railway business is focused on modernizing the wireless communication infrastructure used in safety-critical train telemetry, using the dot16 protocol. These upgrades enable safer operations (better detection of issues, more reliable and timely data), especially as trains grow longer and the demands for safety, regulation, and operational efficiency increase.

Reliable Long-Distance Communication:
Ensures consistent, low-latency links between head-of-train and end-of-train devices, critical for safety on increasingly long freight trains

Enhanced Safety Features: Supports positive train location, integrity monitoring, and real-time brake/telemetry data — directly aligned with DoT safety mandates.
	
Stronger Cybersecurity: Modern encryption and authentication protect against spoofing and signal interference, addressing federal concerns over critical infrastructure resilience.
	
Future-Proof Standards: Based on IEEE 802.16t (dot16), an open, industry-backed standard chosen by the Association of American Railroads, making it scalable, interoperable, and regulator-friendly.

### Post #141: 2025-10-07 16:37 — `$ONDS` `[Dd]`

**Today’s Lesson in Dilution**

$ONDS announced closing of its 425 million dollar public offering today. What’s different about this dilution is the market maker who purchased it a day after listing bought it for a premium (more than the current share price) 

Quick write up of how it works:

-The company issues new shares to raise capital.
-The underwriters agree to buy the new shares at a set price from the company.
-The underwriter then resells these shares to the public or institutions at a slightly higher price — that spread is their profit.

If the stock dips below the offering price immediately after the dilution, the market maker may step in and buy shares to:
-Support the price (stabilization),
-Prevent panic selling,
-And protect the reputation of the diluted company

What’s interesting here is the purchaser hasn’t gotten to layer their shares yet because instead of the offering being below the current price it’s above. This is almost a sure sign we will see the stock at atleast 11.50 in the next week. 

The market maker wants their money. 

Be patient. 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/Drone_Daddy/36L/todays-lesson-in-dilution*

### Post #151: 2025-10-09 19:35 — `$ONDS` `[Dd]`

**$ONDS 1.5B make-or-break**

Very busy today but saw some comments about this. Disclaimer: This post is pulled from the $ONDS sub Reddit from user Alywan and is not my original work but I thought it was a solid read for shareholders. My take: This could be huge and would turn them into a premier NATO defense contractor. 

Ondas Holdings ($ONDS) just filed a Preliminary Proxy Statement (PRE 14A) asking shareholders to vote on doubling its authorized share count from 400 million to 800 million.
This might sound like dry corporate paperwork — but it’s actually one of the most important moments in the company’s history.

If the proposal passes, Ondas could unlock up to $1.478 billion in cash through warrant exercises. If it fails, those warrants become permanently worthless.

This is a classic binary event: either rocket fuel or a dilution bomb.

⸻

🧾 What Shareholders Are Voting On
	1.	Double authorized shares from 400 M → 800 M.
	2.	Increase the employee stock option pool.

⸻

💡 Why This Matters

Earlier this year, Ondas raised $425 million in a public offering.
That deal came with 73.92 million warrants priced at $20 per share.

Here’s the catch: Ondas doesn’t currently have enough authorized shares to issue those warrants if investors choose to exercise them. Without more authorized shares, those warrants remain worthless paper.

⸻

📊 The Math
	•	Current shares outstanding: ≈ 349 million (as of record date)
	•	If all warrants are exercised: + 73.92 million new shares
	•	Potential cash raised: ≈ $1.478 billion

⸻

🟢 Bull Case (Vote Passes)
	•	Ondas gets a $1.5 billion war chest for acquisitions, R&D, and expansion.
	•	The stock would need to reach $20+ for the warrants to be “in the money.”
	•	Dilution occurs — but the company could transform from a small-cap to a major defense contractor.
	•	Investors trade some ownership percentage for a much stronger, better-funded company.

⸻

🔴 Bear Case (Vote Fails)
	•	Warrants expire worthless.
	•	The company remains limited to its existing $425 million in cash.
	•	The stock likely crashes as the growth narrative collapses.
	•	Heavy selling pressure expected from disappointed warrant holders.

⸻

🧠 Alywan’s Take

This vote will likely pass — management has recommended a “YES,” they hold substantial personal stakes, and the move makes business sense.

But it’s essentially binary:

Either ONDS becomes a $2 billion+ defense player with deep pockets,
or it stays a mid-cap with capped growth potential.

For current holders, this is a make-or-break moment.
The result of this vote could determine whether ONDS trades at $8–10 or $15–20+ over the next 12–24 months.

⸻

💬 Bottom Line
	•	Vote yes, and you’re betting on growth, government contracts, and a long-term defense expansion story.
	•	Vote no, and you’re betting dilution risk outweighs the upside.

Either way, this is a pivotal turning point — the kind that defines whether ONDS becomes a household name in U.S. defense tech or remains just another speculative small-cap.

⸻

Positions: Long $ONDS (entered at lower levels, holding through the vote)
Disclaimer: Not financial advice. Do your own due diligence.

*Link: https://afterhour.com/Drone_Daddy/4GG/dollaronds-15b-make-or-break*

### Post #186: 2025-10-27 19:48 — `$ONDS` `[Dd]`

**What 4M Defense Aquisition means for $ONDS**

$ONDS acquired a controlling stake in 4M Defense, an Israeli firm specializing in smart demining and land-intelligence technology. This is yet another revenue stream to add to their tool belt that already included defense and infrastructure with a sizeable addressable market.

What Is 4M Defense?

Founded in 2012, 4M Defense is not your typical defense contractor. Their focus is on “Land Intelligence” - using data, sensors, and automation to locate and remove mines, IEDs, and unexploded ordnance faster, cheaper, and safer.

Their tech stack includes:
-Terrestrial Intelligence Platform (TIP): Combines satellite, drone, and subsurface data to map buried threats up to 4× faster than traditional methods.
-Autonomous Robotics: Platforms like the Golem Detector and Digger D-250 perform high-risk demining missions remotely.
-Aerial Magnetometers: Drone-mounted systems like MagArrow II detect metallic threats from above.

With compliance under NATO and IMAS standards, and registration in the U.S. SAM system, 4M is already cleared to bid for global defense.

Market Opportunity

The global land-mine remediation market is massive. The UN estimates 110 million+ mines remain buried worldwide, with the Ukraine demining effort alone projected to cost $34 billion. 

Just another massive addressable market for $ONDS to enter in its growth phase. 


*Link: https://afterhour.com/Drone_Daddy/7I8/what-4m-defense-aquisition-mea*

### Post #195: 2025-11-03 00:13 — `$LPTH, $DPRO, $ONDS, $UMAC` `[Discuss]`

**This Weeks Preview**

Hello Everyone! I have been quiet this weekend because I was in the process of moving! All of the hard work is done and my work/life balance has received a hefty upgrade due to my commute going from 40 to 3 minutes 😎. This means I should be able to post more and keep you updated on all plays new and old. 

I am quite excited this week for a few reasons. 

1. End to the shutdown - Odds are increasing that we could see an end to the shutdown in the next 1-2 weeks. This is a must for anything defense related as many items outside of the necessities are frozen in the pipleline directly hurting a few of our favorites $LPTH $ONDS $UMAC $DPRO . The general retail sentiment around defense stocks should lighten when this is over. 

2. FOMC decision - we got a .25% rate cut which is great but the real kicker was the fed announcing the end of quantitative tightening. This will allow more money to flow into equities and riskier securities which should benefit our favorite small caps that have been making us so much money until recently.

3. Time of year - We are moving into a historical best time of the year for the stock market. It’s also small cap season and the money currently flowing into large cap and mag 7 earnings plays should rotate into small caps. It’s a great time for the Russell. 

4. Earnings in the next 2 weeks - $ONDS $LPTH and $UMAC with all be reporting earnings in 2 weeks. Even though the companies are not profitable there are some significant tailwinds upon us for anything drone related. Contracts are in the works both domestic and abroad and I expect the guidance to be superb as all 3 of these companies have fantastic CEOs. DJI is quietly being pushed out aswell.

5. China - negotiations have been done and we have some concrete deals on the table with more to come. China is not going to restrict their rare earth materials which is key to some of my plays production. This does not concern me with $LPTH at all as we don’t get germanium from them anyway and a US based alternative will be sought after and pursued aggressively in the coming months/years. 

Overall I’m quite bullish and expect us to close with green seas ahead barring any major news into year end. After I finish putting the finishing touches on my office I’ll be able to complete my drafts on full scope DD for my main positions and a few you may not know much about yet - but you will. 

See you all tomorrow :)

You are being blessed with all the DD today. Hopefully it raises your spirits in the Red Sea of Wallstreet and adds to your conviction as it does mine.

They can take these drones down yes but whoever is sending them can just keep sending more. They use these drones because it allows them to complete their objective without needing to get close to do the dirty work. 

What if I told you there was a solution to this that not only neutralized the drone but also told you where the drone operator controlling it was precisely located and where it originated from? WELL THERE IS. 

Sentrycs technology is a literal game changer for $ONDS . Instead of just neutralizing the ammunition you are able to also get at the user creating the threat and law enforcement will be able to send a FAST response team to deal with the antagonist. They also get the drone specs, where the camera was positions, and serial numbers/where it was produced. This tech is mobile and can be used while actively moving in a vehicle or left in a static position. 

This is huge for any pop up sporting events, political rallies, concerts or other large social gatherings where bad people are trying to hurt as many civilians as they can. 

The era of anonymity hiding behind a screen far away is over before it largely began. This will make it much harder for drug dealers, terrorists, and criminals to get away with their plots without retribution. 

Sentrycs already had a 1 pager on their website for airport security. They are prepared. This is my favorite addition to $ONDS ever.

### Post #211: 2025-11-07 16:06 — `$LPTH, $ONDS, $UMAC` `[News]`

**US Army to buy 1 MILLION Drones**

We knew this was coming. Ukraine proved that the mass production of cheap drones was the solution to more expensive and unruly equipment. A couple hundred dollar drone could destroy a million dollar tank. Countries noticed.

The US has been behind China in the drone race for sometime - that changes now and the army doesn’t just want the normal cream of the crop. They want innovators.

### Post #222: 2025-11-10 18:48 — `$LPTH, $ONDS, $LMT, $UMAC` `[Dd]`

**$UMAC Earnings Call Key Takeaways. 5x projected annual Revenue Potential.**

Here’s a couple bullet points for Q3 data followed by my insights that I don’t see getting coverage anywhere.

-First profitable quarter ever
-6 consecutive quarters of revenue growth with year over year increase of 39%
-130 million cash on the balance sheet
-16 million in new purchase orders to be complete my Q2 2026
-Over 50% of revenue was from enterprise entities instead of retail including the Army and Large Private Defense Contractors who are servicing the department of War. 
-new fulfillment center and ability to continue operation for order fulfillment when gov shutdown ends. 
-on track to become consistently profitable in 2026 with at least 30 million in annual revenue. 

Overall a fantastic quarter. 

What really sparked my interest were 2 questions posed by institutional analysts at the end of the call:

The first surrounded Sky Foundry and the Army’s PBAS programs. They asked hypothetically what $UMAC could produce if one of these big programs put in an order. On top of the 20 million in orders they are already working they said they’d pre positioned enough cap ex to fulfill 100-150 million dollars of components or 5x their current annual revenue goal. As a footnote - to do this for 2026 would require them knowing orders were coming by EOY 2025 Allan Evans ($UMAC CEO) said. It sounded like he was indirectly addressing the leaders of those programs in the call which isn’t surprising because $UMAC is already directly contracted by the army.

The second thing I found interesting was Allan bringing up Sam Rubin ($LPTH CEO) and foreshadowing a larger partnership. The 2 companies have facilities only 20 minutes apart in Florida and are in an investment triangle with $ONDS. Allan brought up the fact that it’s possible in late 2026 that $UMAC could be producing $LPTHs infrared germanium free cameras. If $UMAC could scale the production of these cameras to work through the $LPTH backlog at a faster, more affordable rate the sky is the limit. It’s likely many large defense contractors like $LMT and L3 Harris currently seeking germanium made infrared would swap to a cheaper, domestic partner over foreign suppliers. $ONDS as an early investor would be a primary beneficiary. 

A final few thoughts -

I think $UMAC has a lot to gain on the retail side of things too. DJI who owns 70% of the retail drone market is being pushed out by the FCC but the market for drones is only increasing. I think we will see an uptick in $UMAC component purchase orders for civilian drones and drones used for corporate ventures unrelated to defense in Q4 2025 increasing exponentially over the next 5 years.

Allan mentioned that $UMAC is the only domestic company fulfilling thousands of drone motor purchase orders and other start ups are being killed by the shutdown while $UMACs healthy balance sheet is keeping them going. End to the shutdown = large rally.

We have good numbers, we have tangible speculation, and I expect big gains by the time 2026 is underway. 

More to come.

### Post #230: 2025-11-12 22:30 — `$LHX, $LPTH, $ONDS, $LMT` `[Dd]`

**$LPTH Earning Calls Breakdown and Key Takeaways**

The $LPTH earnings call yesterday was about as good as it could have been. This was indicated by the sharp share price uptick over $9 during the call. Unfortunately we fell hard today - in this market environment where the longest shutdown in history is putting defense plays in stasis on the contract side. That is not stopping the work.

Here are my key take aways - (pay extra attention to 3 and 9)

1. LightPath Technologies Inc reported a 79% increase in revenue for Q1 2026, reaching $15.1 million compared to $8.4 million in the same quarter last year.	

2. The introduction of the Germanium-free G5 camera variant utilizing black diamond glass addresses supply chain concerns and enhances product reliability. Operating expenses increased by 66% to $7 million, primarily due to the integration of G5 and INCREASED sales and marketing spending.

3. Lockheed Martin ($LMT) per $LPTH CEO SAM RUBIN is “Very Bullish” on them winning the contract and is already pre producing components for the missile system out of their expanding Texas vertical integration facility. This would produce 50-100 MILLION annual recurring revenue.

4. 2 more missile program contracts are in talks. Ground work already laid due to Lockheed quadstar testing causing a faster turn around for development (possibly in 2026).

5. 7-8 - 10 million dollar +*  recurring annual revenue deals in different stages of planning and fulfillment.

6. Black diamond glass is reported to be better than germanium products from repeat customers further fueling repeat business. They have a focus on marketing and selling this point. 

7. Contract with L3 Harris ($LHX) is expected to enter low-rate initial production in the coming months, with full integration on US NAVAL destroyers targeted by 2027.

8. IT Infrastructure Upgrades - Migrated to a new provider to meet high-level defense cybersecurity requirements in the fourth quarter. (They’d only need this if they needed to produce for these entities.) 

9. BIGGEST UPDATE OUTSIDE OF $LMT - LightPath expects that there will be over 1,000 probably 1200~ new border surveillance towers installed and they ultimately expect to WIN PLACEMENT IN THE MAJORITY of those with prices of $150,000-$250,000 per camera. Taking the average that would be 200 MILLION in revenue

10. More than $15 million of our current backlog is for Counter-UAS applications. I believe this to be related to $ONDS and their upcoming contracts

If we take best case scenario that’s 
100 million - LMT
200 million - boarder 
80+ million - misc contracts 
20+ million - CUAS

Totals to 400 million in annual revenue equivalents with most of that being recurring revenue. Their market cap is only 350 million currently. 

They also got a recent PT upgrade to $10

Current bottlenecks are more workforce required to work through large 90+ million backlog. 

Very Bullish from my perspective.

### Post #246: 2025-11-15 04:09 — `$LPTH, $PLTR, $ONDS, $UMAC` `[Dd]`

**$ONDS Full Earnings Breakdown and Key Takeaways**

Alright $ONDS investors, lurkers, and friends here’s your required reading before bed try not get too excited (I won’t blame you if you do it’s good stuff). 

All of the points are relevant but I think #1 is the biggest by far. It took me awhile to write this so if you read the whole thing I’d appreciate it - it’s for your benefit after all!

1. 2025 revenue target increased to at least $36 million; establishing preliminary revenue target of at least $110 million for 2026. 

So we got our guidance raised to 110 million for 2026 which is great but if you were paying attention to the earnings call you’d notice that they snuck something in there - the potential for 500 million in revenue for 2026 dependent on mergers and acquisitions. If this happens that is nearly 5x the current guidance - insanity considering revenue was only in the 10s of millions this year. They are playing it conservatively and I don’t think they would put that bullet point on there unless they thought it was possible. If they even get 200 or 300 million in earnings the projections will sky rocket. I don’t toss this around lightly but we could be looking at the $PLTR of the drone trend. 

2. Entered into a definitive agreement with Sentrycs, an Israeli counter-UAS company specializing in RF-based drone detection, identification, and mitigation technologies. We believe Sentrycs' systems will provide passive, signal-intelligence-driven protection against intruder drones, complementing Iron Drone Raider and expanding OAS' layered counter-UAS architecture across detection, interception, and electronic defense.

This was truly the cherry on top for 2025. We needed a software solution for CUAS tech to bundle with the Iron Drone Raider. If you check airbotics linked in you’ll see one of their employees mentioned they are selling CUAS bundles in association with other companies for the World Cup - a 1 billion dollar opportunity. Adding Sentrycs to the mix makes them an extremely strong contender to win a portion of that contract. Not to mention the one they are winning with DHS. 
 
3. Record quarterly revenue of $10.1 million in Q3; a more than 6-fold increase YoY and 60% growth QoQ
4. With a pro-forma cash balance of $840.4 million, we believe Ondas has one of the strongest balance sheets in the industry. 

Ive never seen a company this small with a balance sheet like this. Nearly a billion dollars in firepower but they’d need that to produce 500 million in revenue with M and A which is why I believe it’s possible.

4. OAS backlog reached $22.2 million as of September 30, 2025, up from $20.7 million at the end of Q2, reflecting continued global demand for the Optimus and Iron Drone platforms.

I expect this to grow further with the Sentrycs acquisition and I believe it will positively effect $UMAC and $LPTH. Two positions of mine that are beat down but will recover with an $ONDS surge.

5. Secured GreenUAS Cleared List certification for the Optimus drone, positioning the system for Department of War Blue List inclusion as a trusted, NDAA-compliant autonomous platform.

This is obviously big and we will see ourselves added to the blue UAS list for Optimus and iron drone systems though it may be in 2026 

6. Appointed Maj. Gen. (Ret.) Yoav Har-Even, former CEO of Rafael Advanced Defense Systems, to the OAS Advisory Board, adding exceptional global defense leadership and operational experience to guide the scaling of OAS operations and support the Company's strategic acquisition program.

Rafael made the iron dome. Expect $ONDS to be involved in the US Golden Dome with advisors like this.

7. Iron Drone Raider was selected by Securiton Germany, a leading integration partner to the German Armed Forces and other critical security operators, following successful system integration and demonstrations conducted in Germany by Airobotics. The joint demonstrations showcased Iron Drone's advanced counter-UAS capabilities and seamless interoperability within Securiton's defense and security frameworks.

Proof of concept. I expect $ONDS contracts from NATO allies with this endorsement. Expect this tech at European airports and other countries around the globe with the Sentrycs acquisition - a perfect use case. Their tech is being used already in 180 + areas.

8. The Association of American Railroads (AAR) Wireless Communications Committee (WCC) announced in September that IEEE 802.16t ("dot16") will serve as the upgrade path for the legacy 160 MHz LMR voice network, joining the 900 MHz and 450 MHz networks where dot16 has already been formally adopted.

Doesn’t get the attention of Ondas Autonomous systems but important all the same. They have a diversified product portfolio and are global. People underestimate the potential of this company. I will not.

If you made it this far - know that you are awesome and I have something special for my followers coming soon. If you got to the bottom let me know with a 💎 

😉❤️

### Post #263: 2025-11-19 18:52 — `$ONDS` `[Dd]`

**A Look Behind the Curtain of what could be $ONDS (I think it’s a lot)**

Before I get into some exciting DD if you want the chance to win my guide on how I do what I do for free make sure to enter the contest I posted about yesterday. Entry’s close tonight at 6pm CST. 1 entry per person and winners announced tonight! 

Following the special shareholder meeting tomorrow for $ONDS I have a special announcement of my own so keep an eye out. 👀💎

DD TIMEEE: 

I was looking into Mistral Inc. more after their recently brokered deal with the US on a nearly $1 billion dollar drone contract fulfilled by Uvision. For such a well connected company they don’t have much information posted about them publicly and are not active on social media - that’s because they don’t need to be. 

I’m not sure what kind of stranglehold they have on the US government behind the scenes but when they market a product the fed buys. 

I previously let you know they partnered with $ONDS to sell their tech and don’t have the $ONDS iron drone raider under their CUAV solutions on the website yet. That doesn’t mean they aren’t brokering contracts in private. 

The one post they made about it listed it as their “State of the Art TRL-9 C-UAS. TRL-9 is a technology designation for top of the line “gold standard” product. Here’s a brief write up explaining what makes something TRL-9.

TRL-9 = System proven in operational mission conditions.

For the Pentagon, NATO, DHS, DoT, FAA, and every major defense integrator, TRL-9 remains the highest and most trusted validation level because it means:
-Fielded in real-world operations
-Survived actual mission environments (weather, interference, contested airspace, adversarial UAS)
-Achieved successful recurring performance, not just tests
-Certifiable and deployable for procurement programs

No framework has replaced TRL, and no alternative metric has become more widely adopted.
If a C-UAS system claims TRL-9, it signals mature, combat-ready tech.

Mistral Inc is marketing it for what it is - cutting edge tech. 

Now let’s take a look at who Mistral is marketing to and what for in my attachments. 

They work directly with:
-the DoW (DoD)
-DHS
-Other Federal Agencies

They offer services for:
-transportation hubs: airports, urban infrastructure, etc
-public events: stadiums for sporting events, concerts, large gatherings

We have 500 million on the table for DHS contracts and 500 million for the World Cup. This does not include international interest and domestic interest from the DoT and FAA for airport C-UAS needs. Potentially a billion dollar business. You better believe Mistral is working hard behind the scenes to make this happen along with the $ONDS team. They chose $ONDS because the product is good and use case better. 

I don’t care what $ONDS does daily. I’m big picture. The thesis just keeps getting more bullish.

### Post #267: 2025-11-20 16:53 — `$LPTH, $DPRO, $ONDS, $UMAC` `[Dd]`

**How to make money and position yourself in stocks that are falling using technical analysis**

Footnote: 💎FOR THOSE WHO DIDNT WIN A GUIDE MAKE SURE TO READ TILL THE END💎

Lots of comments sent to me today about why $ONDS is falling off good news. Volatility Seems to be the story with this gem as of late. 

Due Diligence and Research is my passion but every good investor needs to implement some from of TA (Technical Analysis) if you want to identify good entries/exits, short term plays, and trends. What you may not know about me as the “DD guy” is that I used to be a registered representative security licensed through FINRA and the SEC. 

You could apply what I’m about to show you to any position but I thought id stay on brand and take a look at the $ONDS monthly chart and what entry I’m targeting with my cash. Enough yapping let’s get into it. 

Looking at the attached Trading View chart can be very overstimulating without an explanation of what’s going on. 

I am using 2 indicators: 

RSI (Relative Strength Index) shown at the bottom: RSI shows me what is overbought and oversold. If RSI goes below 30 (oversold) or above 70 (overbought) it gives me a good idea of where the price may go. 

Lux Algo’s Fair Value Gap Indicator: I have bullish and bearish fair value gaps. These are more complicated than RSI and are created when a 3 candlestick pattern moved rapidly and leaves a “gap” typically it’s a point price will retest after a grab on liquidity to “fill it”. I like them on trading view because they also usually proceed a trend continuation on larger time frames. 

The only other things I added were the blue lines marking highs and lows in a recent trend to the upside. I also added a green circle where an order block has formed on the weekly. Price is drawn to this level and we are currently seeing a liquidity sweep back to this range. I am targeting this point for my entry. 

I am waiting for price to retrace into the $6.30 - $6.50 range. Once it does I will sell CSPs at or slightly below the money for next week looking for a bounce off the order block. Highly Volatile stocks have higher implied volatility which equals more premium for the seller (me). 

You follow me for DD but I am more than just that. Is this something you’d like to see more of for $ONDS , $UMAC, $LPTH , $DPRO etc? 

Only 2% of those who entered got my guide. It’d be a waste if everyone didn’t have access to learn how I do what I do. I’ll be giving you guys a peak at one of my favorite chapters tonight. Investing is a mindset. Huge announcement coming  at 7cst 💎. Stay tuned.

“Countering Mini-Drones: armasuisse procures drone-defense systems from Swiss supplier Securiton

Body:

The Federal Office for Defence Procurement armasuisse has awarded the contract for systems to counter mini-drones. After a four-week evaluation test (field trial) for the detection and defense of mini-drones to protect military infrastructure at Meiringen Air Base, armasuisse commissioned the Swiss supplier Securiton to deliver several drone-defense systems.

The systems are partially mobile and can also be used in support operations by the Swiss Army on behalf of civilian authorities.

The Army already has experience with the use of mini-drones. These are primarily used for reconnaissance and surveillance tasks to obtain information for better situational assessment. However, there is currently a capability gap in defending against hostile mini-drones.

⸻

🇨🇭 Translation 2 — Securiton AG Post

👏 We are proud that armasuisse has awarded us the contract to deliver drone-defense systems. After successful field tests, we are now permitted to provide several partially mobile systems to support the Swiss Army in protecting its infrastructure and troops.”

**Department of Homeland Security Announces $1.5 BILLION C-UAS Request for Proposal Due December 8th $ONDS**

From @ m5ive on X. 

“On Friday 11/21, the Department of Homeland Security released its Counter UAS RFP.  There is $1.5B of contract awards being made available under this program to cover the entire agency. Offers are due December 8 with awards to come soon thereafter.  

Background:

The Department of Homeland Security (DHS) requires Counter-Unmanned Aircraft Systems (C- UAS) solutions to address the growing threats posed by unauthorized or malicious unmanned aircraft systems (UAS). These systems must support the detection, tracking, locating, classification, identification, and mitigation of UAS threats across diverse operational
environments. This procurement will enable DHS components to acquire adaptable C-UAS capabilities tailored to their unique mission requirements, ensuring the protection of critical infrastructure, public safety, and national security. As part of its mission, DHS is responsible for securing National Special Security Events (NSSE) and Special Event Assessment Rating (SEAR) events, which include high-profile gatherings
such as presidential inaugurations, major sporting events, and other large-scale public events. These events often require specialized airspace security measures to address UAS threats and ensure the safety of attendees, critical infrastructure, and surrounding communities. C-UAS solutions procured under this effort will play a vital role in supporting DHS’s ability to secure these events and respond to evolving threats.” End thread.

### Post #277: 2025-11-23 19:02 — `$ONDS` `[Dd]`

**I Completed Field Research Last Night $ONDS**

I am a music lover and listen to everything (except Taylor Swift - sorry ladies). I jam out in the car, lifting, researching, working, etc. One one of my favorite hobbies is going to concerts. I had one Friday and one Saturday. Last night something caught my eye circling the venue 15 ft above me. I captured some shots for you below. 

It’s common in 2025 for artists to hire drone pilots to take videos of the crowd for their marketing - I don’t blame them the shots are amazing. 

I’m more concerned with venue security - here’s why. I’m the type of guy (Big Boy 6’2” 215lbs) that loves to be in the action (General Admisission). For those that don’t know GA is the section of a venue that’s right in front of the stage. There is no seating and everyone is packed shoulder to shoulder together. If someone was able to smuggle a lethal drone inside there would be no time for anyone to react. From what I observed they had no way to combat a threat like this and sadly I don’t think most stadiums/venues will until something deadly happens and there’s a reactive response instead of preparing proactively. 

A singular Iron Drone Box and a Sentrycs system is all it would take to keep this active venue safe from that threat. 

I am going to CC Mr. Brock (CEO $ONDS) on X about this opportunity in my city and may dig deeper into it during our AH interview. Sentrycs is already servicing concert venues but I want to see how much I can get done personally to stop dangerous situations before they happen.

### Post #296: 2025-11-27 18:16 — `$ONDS` `[Dd]`

**Dozens of Federal Agencies Initiate Counter-UAS Collaboration. Happy Thanksgiving!**

The U.S. government just took a major step toward formalizing how it defends airspace against unauthorized and hostile drones.

More than 180 leaders and technical experts from across the federal government recently convened to launch a coordinated counter-UAS collaboration effort, bringing together roughly 50 agencies under a unified national framework. The initiative is being led through a newly activated joint interagency task force focused specifically on small unmanned aircraft system (sUAS) threats.

This is not theoretical planning. This is operational coordination.

The group outlined three clear priorities:

1. Domestic Protection of Critical Regions and Events
The National Capital Region, the U.S. southern border, and major international events like the 2026 World Cup are being prioritized as high-risk airspace environments. These locations represent real-world testing grounds for persistent drone detection, identification, and mitigation systems.

2. Warfighter Enablement Overseas
Small drones have become one of the most disruptive force multipliers in modern conflict. This task force aims to rapidly integrate counter-UAS capabilities that can protect troops, logistics hubs, and forward operating bases.

3. Nationwide Training & Intelligence Sharing
Federal, state, local, and tribal agencies will now be pulling from the same playbook. That means shared sensor data, standardized procedures, and interoperable counter-drone infrastructure across jurisdictions.

Drone threats are now a permanent part of U.S. national security planning — and the response is being systematized at scale.

Why is this good for my drone ETF (mainly $ONDS) ?

Their C-UAS tech can be specced for mobility. Sentrycs COFR can be put into vehicles - ideal for mobile forward operating bases. The iron drone raider comes in a box and can be transported to. 

Now we have roboteams UGV’s to boot that could carry this systems where they need to go. 

We are looking at the future right now. A fully mechanized autonomous war machine. I am excited but this is getting a little too close to sky net 🤣.

Happy thanksgiving folks! ❤️

### Post #299: 2025-11-28 17:51 — `$ONDS` `[Dd]`

**$ONDS in talks to acquire Israeli Stealth Startup founded my members of legendary Unit 81. Valuation Estimates in hundreds of millions.**

This is a long one so strap in. 

There’s a name floating around Israeli defense tech circles that hasn’t yet made it onto most U.S. investors’ radar:

Oz — a stealth Israeli startup focused on stopping friendly fire.

According to Hebrew financial reporting, Ondas is in talks to acquire Oz for what’s described as a “hundreds of millions of dollars” ballpark deal. That would put it in the same weight class as Ondas’ Sentrycs acquisition and squarely in the “strategic cornerstone” bucket, not a bolt-on tuck-in. I’ve reiterated many times how important Sentrycs was to the C-UAS Thesis.

“DD why is this different than other M&As we’ve had recently?” 

Let me show you.

What Oz Actually Does

Oz is not “another drone company” or a generic AI defense startup. The company is building technology to prevent friendly fire / two-sided shooting on modern battlefields. They’re trying to solve one of the ugliest, most politically explosive, and technically brutal problems in warfare:

How do you make sure your own forces don’t accidentally kill each other in a chaotic, multi-domain fight?

During the early stages of the recent Gaza war, friendly fire was a material part of IDF casualties. Israeli media has reported that in the early phase, a meaningful chunk of fallen soldiers weren’t killed by Hamas, but by their own side in the fog of war. Oz was founded specifically in the shadow of that reality, by people who saw the problem firsthand and came back from reserve duty determined to fix it.  

From what’s been disclosed:

•Sector: Defense tech, military market only
•Product theme: Tech to prevent “two-sided shooting” (friendly-fire incidents)  
•Model: B2B / B2G (military, defense primes, governments)  
•Funding: ~$10.5M raised in a seed round in late 2024, led by serious local funds like TLV Partners and other well-known angels.  

They are currently operating underground no fancy website, no product one-pager, no “AI for Defense” marketing. That alone tells you who the real customer is: not retail businesses, not LinkedIn, but operational units.

Who Runs Oz?

Per the Hebrew reporting article I translated Oz was founded by:

•Uri Abraham
•Uri Ashur
•Matan Cohen

All three are described as veterans of Unit 81, the Israeli military’s black-box technology unit. Before Oz, they reportedly ran a robotics department within Israel’s official weapons and technology R&D directorate—basically the place where new classified systems are born, tested, and iterated with real combat units.

These are individuals that have experienced war their whole lives.

•Lived with real operational constraints
•Seen real friendly-fire risk up close
•Built systems that have to work the first time, in real combat, not just in a demo

So when they say “we’re going to reduce friendly fire,” they’re not spitballing a feature idea. They’re trying to operationalize what they already know from the inside.

Why Unit 81 Is a Big Deal

To understand why Oz is special, you have to understand Unit 81.

Unit 81 is the secret technology unit inside the Special Operations Division of Israeli Military Intelligence. Its entire mission is to build custom, highly classified tech for combat units and intelligence operatives—hardware, software, sensors, comms, gadgets, you name it.  

A few key points:

•It’s often described as the most elite hardware/tech unit in the IDF, the “boutique R&D shop” compared to Unit 8200’s mass-scale cyber and signals work.  
•Extremely selective: tens of thousands of students are initially screened each year; only a few hundred make it in.  
•Alumni have founded dozens of startups with billions in total funding and >$10B in cumulative valuations—some of the most sophisticated computer vision, cyber, and defense-adjacent companies in Israel trace back to Unit 81 leadership.  

In other words:

If 8200 is the “Israeli NSA,” then I don’t think it’s crazy to say that Unit 81 is the skunkworks lab that builds the toys—from advanced sensors to bespoke systems for special operations.

So when you see: “Oz was founded by Unit 81 alumni who ran a robotics department in Israel’s weapons R&D ecosystem”, that immediately places them in the highest-trust, highest-capability tier of Israeli defense tech.

This isn’t random talent. This is peak pipeline.

Why Friendly Fire Is an A-Tier Problem

Friendly fire isn’t some edge case. In dense urban and hybrid warfare—tunnels, drones, armor, infantry, EW, and air support all stacked together—ID, tracking, and de-confliction become nightmares.

You get:

•Multiple units converging on the same target from different angles
•Air assets and ground assets overlapping fields of fire
•Drones feeding video from unclear perspectives
•Split-second decisions with incomplete IFF (Identification Friend or Foe)

One mistake you can’t take back and you’re firing on:

•Your own infantry
•Another friendly armored column
•Special ops teams already in the compound

Operationally horrific, Politically disastrous, Morally devastating.

So any tech that can reduce that risk:

•Protects soldiers
•Reduces political blowback
•Makes high-tempo operations more sustainable
•Becomes a must-have system, not a “nice to have” gadget

That’s exactly the category Oz is aiming at.

Why Oz Would Be a Monster Fit Inside Ondas

Ondas today is stitching together a multi-domain autonomy and sensing stack:
•Autonomous drones (American Robotics, Airobotics)
•Counter-UAS (Sentrycs and Iron Drone Raider)
•Smart demining and ground robotics (4M Defense, Roboteam, Apeiro Motion)  

They already own:

-The platforms (drones + UGVs)
-The counter-drone layer (Sentrycs)

What they don’t fully have yet is the decision layer specifically tuned to preventing human-on-human friendly fire across all those assets and inevitable new ones they acquire or create. 

That’s where Oz slots in:

•Take in sensor fusion from ground robots, drones, RF, and EO systems
•Track unit locations, lines of fire, and potential fratricide risk
•Flag or block engagements that look like blue-on-blue
•Potentially integrate into C2 systems to give commanders a real-time “do not shoot” overlay

If Ondas ends up acquiring Oz, you’re not just bolting on another product. You’re basically attaching a “safety OS” for autonomous and semi-autonomous warfare on top of everything they’ve already bought.

That would:

•Deepen Ondas’ moat with mission-critical software, not just hardware
•Make their stack harder to displace once integrated into doctrines and SOPs
•Strengthen their pitch to NATO, U.S., and allied militaries who are all terrified of politically catastrophic friendly-fire events in the drone/AI era

Why This Matters for the Thesis

If this deal closes anywhere near the rumored “hundreds of millions” range, the price alone tells you how important Oz’s tech and team are being treated behind closed doors.

From the thesis perspective:

-Oz is a solution to solve a key problem if Ondas expands their autonomous platforms for combat that fixes a mission-critical problem.
-Ondas + Oz = not just autonomy + sensing, but battlefield de-confliction at the software/AI layer.

That is exactly how you move from “cool drones and robots” into Palantir-style, system-of-systems infrastructure, rooted in hardware, sensing, and real-world robotics instead of just data platforms.

I’d love to see this acquisition go through.

### Post #344: 2025-12-10 01:13 — `$ONDS` `[Discuss]`

**It’s More Than Just Money - Invest In Yourself**

This is a motivational post that i will consider a success if it even changes 1 persons mindset for the better. 

I was talking to one of my friends today after posting my $ONDS investment journey YTD who asked me how I changed so much in the last couple years. 

It caught me off guard and I didn’t really know how to respond because that’s not something we ever talk about - (I’m not a person that really has deep conversations with my friends for better or worse we mainly joke and keep things light).

I didn’t have an answer but now I think I do: 

For years in my early and mid 20s I was living day by day just taking dopamine hits by eating addictive food and playing hours and hours of video games a day. Going to my job that I had no passion for and living for the work day to be over so I could go home and do it all over again. I didn’t have any long term goals. 

I don’t remember how it happened but one day a switch just flipped. I realized I only have 1 life, 1 family, 1 body and I was throwing away some of the best years of my life for nothing. 

I knew I could do amazing things and attain goals I set forth that seemed impossible at the time if I just showed up every day and actually tried.

I started investing in myself - eating right, working out, getting good sleep, making time for family, being more social, and trying to figure out what I wanted to do in life. 

Eventually investing stuck. I absolutely fell in love with the game and everything that goes into it. Because I’ve built good habits and invested myself I’m a genuinely happy, level headed person with a good support structure that can process my market niche with a steady eye culminating in me starting my own company: Hidden Gems Research LLC. 

It wasnt a fast transition. It took years and it was absolutely lonely but it was 100% worth the investment and I’m so grateful that my past self didn’t sell future me short. Like in stock trading - patience and consistency is KEY. 

Here’s my ask of you if you’re feeling stuck - I wish someone was there to tell me this years earlier.  

If you’re looking to make a change this new year with anything - investing, money, diet, family, anything - don’t wait - start tonight. Your future self will thank you. 

Hopefully this helps someone like it helped me. ❤️💎

ONDS DD coming tonight here when I get home PROMISE!!

Face reveal soon? 😆

*Link: https://afterhour.com/Drone_Daddy/bck4/its-more-than-just-money-inves*

### Post #348: 2025-12-11 02:58 — `$ACHR, $LPTH, $TTD, $LULU, $ONDS, $UMAC` `[Discuss]`

**My Option Basics for Making Steady Income on Stocks I Want to Own. 💎**

Over the last couple years of trying out different strategies with options I’ve found that the most successful method for me was actually a combination of multiple. No one size fits all and with a variable market comes a constant need for pivoting between strategies and using the correct one at the right time. If you don’t understand why selling a covered call on a stock that’s red for the day may be a bad idea this guide might help you!

Mine Strategy Stack Includes:

A: Selling Cash Secured Puts on stocks in the red to secure a solid premium and good entry. (Think $TTD when it dropped over 40% in a day this August).

B: Selling Covered Calls on overbought positions that have moved up rapidly and are in the upper bands of RSI (Think $ONDS a couple weeks ago when it moved nearly 30% in a day.)

C. Buying out of the Money LEAPS on long term thesis winners when they’ve recently sold off. (Think $LULU lemon - a company with strong fundamentals and a healthy balance sheet that’s been beaten down this year but is prime for a reversal).

Each of these strategies has a use case and is a tool in my belt when I need it. It takes time to understand it but once you do it will become second nature and has the potential to give you better leverage and lower your breakeven on new investments. 

I explained all 3 of the basics of these strategies with pending Robinhood orders on $ACHR in the FREE section of my options guide on Substack you can read here:

https://substack.com/@hiddengemsresearchllc/note/p-181110129?r=6xxf1q&utm_medium=ios&utm_source=notes-share-action

I realize there are a lot of new investors on this app that could benefit from some options basics like these. Seasoned investors will find value aswell - especially in the second half for the advanced specifics of how I execute the trades themselves. 

My goal is to hit 500 free subscriptions or 50 premium members by X-Mas so I can launch the Gem Vault Portfolio and post all of my trades in an open forum. This will be a huge benefit to visual learners that want real examples from a successful trader (Me!) with a confirmed track record - none of this sketchy bad actor shit we’ve seen on AfterHour recently! My goal is transparency which is why I will be posting all trades and positions for the Gem Vault Portfolio. 

Im Excited to start the new fund using the stock spread I have yet to reveal in full: you better believe some premium beasts with high implied volatility like $ONDS $UMAC and $LPTH will be present. 

It’ll be fun to see where this new port is by the end of 2026 with all of the tailwinds I see ahead/FOMC news today and where the ports are of those who will have joined me. It’s going to be a great year!! 

-DD 💎❤️

### Post #431: 2025-12-31 22:12 `[Discuss]`

**Would you guys be interested in a Video where I talk about Fair Value Gaps and Liquidity?**

I was taking a look at some charts I’m watching on the daily timeframe that both had large quick moves that created fair value gaps. Price action is typically drawn back to these “gaps” because it moved too quickly in one direction or another. FVG retrace meant is one of my favorite smart money concepts in my TA tool belt for figuring out when to take profit and build a position or wait for a new retest. You’ll see in the charts both retested and bounced off these levels.

Starting the year I had the research skills but my TA was lacking. I learned a ton of technical skills this year that helped my trading game immensely - turning a 5 figure cost basis into 6 figure gains (not including cost basis.)

The video would go into FVGs, RSI, and Liquidity sweeps as these indicators are intertwined and are the core of my swing trade strategy. 

If this post gets enough support I’ll work on something this weekend for substack after I drop a full analysis breakdown of my new gem vault index that’s launching Friday.

I wanted to remind new and old investors alike that you do not need options to be successful in today’s market. Many people like to post huge wins where option leverage helped them achieve crazy results to build hype (im guilty of this too). What they don’t show are the trades that didn’t work out where their contracts expired worthless and they lost all of their money - a double edged sword. 

In one of my self directed brokerages From July-December 2025 I was able to take $47,000 to $135,000 (+187%)  just swing trading shares - buying low and selling high. Sure I have less leverage but there’s no risk to time decay as I own the underlying asset but still had option like movement.

Finding the right undervalued stock at the right time is all you need to attain crazy results. Unless you really know what youre doing and have a plan (check out my wave option strategy on Substack if you need one) don’t gamble on options. It only takes 1 good trade.

### Post #436: 2026-01-02 15:57 — `$ONDS` `[Dd]`

**When a Covered Call Doesn’t Work Out**

If you want to be successful In trading, 99% of the time a system is required IF you want to be consistent. 

For me that’s my Options Wave Strategy. Selling CSPs on -10% moves to the downside or Covered calls on +10% daily pops. It also includes buyin LEAPS on CSP (red) days. 

Occasionally a stock really moves like $ONDS right now, capping my upside gains on my covered calls and showing a loss. This is fine - gains are gains and I will let these shares get calls away, I’m after the premium and I was ok with the capped gains when I wrote it. 

I hold a majority of my shares long and uncovered + open the LEAPS to hedge against my capped gains. The large premium sellside calls do the inverse and hedge weekly downturns. 

Symbiosis. This is how you win playing both sides. 

The Gem Vault is live if you want to check out how a profitable trader operates in today’s uncertain market, link to my company’s website in my bio! 💎

*Link: https://afterhour.com/Drone_Daddy/bdW9/when-a-covered-call-doesnt-wor*

### Post #485: 2026-01-09 18:21 — `$ONDS` `[Dd]`

**Here is a Primary Example of why you never cover all of your shares IF you are bullish on a company like me with $ONDS + non financial advice for you! 💎**

I have large portion of capital set aside for long term investments that are pure uncovered shares.

I have another port designed to aggressively generate premium using my wave strategy on small caps with high IV meaning high premiums. This bucket includes stocks I like long term but I’m not worried about keeping these. This strategy plays on volatility and reacts after a large daily move - it wins a lot BUT not always. See provided meme worthy screenshot. 

 $ONDS was in the $8s less than 2 weeks ago and rose to the $9s 12/30 - a strong move so I sold covered calls on the 300 shares I’ve been wheeling since November. No one could have predicted the move to over $15 dollars as of yesterday. This trade capped my gains so the only thing I’m losing out on is additional upside I won’t be seeing as I will let the shares get called away. A win is a win just much less winning than doing nothing here. LUCKILY I own thousands of shares uncovered. You’ll find a trend on my profile - I always have positions hedging other ones. 

I think this screenshot is a great visual reminder to seperate port allocation based on objective and stick to your Strategy. It could just as easily go the other way and those covered called generate 100% premium for you. 

I am not perfect and will not win every trade. My goal is to win most - even if its capped gains, while rarely losing. That is how I’ve been able to consistently win throughout 2025. I got euphoric in October and was humbled - that won’t happen again. All contigencies are in place and gains set to automatically become realized should an unforeseen event occur. 

Keep sponging information from the great people on here, keep learning, dont make the same mistake twice, stay humble and you WILL get where you want to be. 

*Link: https://afterhour.com/Drone_Daddy/beH6/here-is-a-primary-example-of-w*

### Post #529: 2026-01-17 03:57 — `$ONDS` `[Dd]`

**$ONDS Investors Day Highlights from @BMSInvests on X.**

I wanted to get something out on paper for people who weren’t able to attend investors day today but saw this and knew it would be a more details write up. If your invested in $ONDS and don’t follow this guy you’re making a mistake. He finds a lot of breaking news first and has a good grasp of the domestic drone landscape and policy. 

My take before the write up: $ONDS is lowballing their current guidance for surprise revenue beats. They haven’t included their massive border projects in the current backlog. Could absolutely continue current downward momentum into February without a catalyst so I am saving additional assets to pivot into an aggressive $ONDS strategy should we see anything lower than $10. On to the post. 

Current Financials

•  Backlog: $65.3 million as of December 31, 2025 — (+180% from $23.3 million on November 13, 2025). Very strong momentum with multiple customers and more mature programs contributing.
•  Pro Forma Cash Position: Exceeds $1.5 billion (year-end 2025, adjusted for the ~$1 billion recent registered direct offering closed in mid-January 2026).
•  Revenue Performance:
    •  Q4 2025 preliminary: $27.0–$29.0 million (~578% YoY growth; 51% above prior internal Q4 target).
    •  FY 2025 preliminary: $47.6–$49.6 million (~576% YoY growth; 23% above prior full-year target, including Roboteam contribution closed in December 2025).
•  FY 2026 Revenue Target: $170–$180 million (conservative; +25% raised from prior $140 million guidance; excludes any M&A contributions).
•  Investment Gain in Public Companies: $11.4 million unrealised gains.
•  PDW Investment: Expected to deliver strategic collaborations over time.

Current Position

•  Customers and partners prefer well-funded suppliers that can reliably meet targets and deliver at scale.
•  Conservative TAM/SAM (2025–2030): ~$117 billion TAM; ~$7.5 billion SAM.
•  Iron Drone: Involved in several sensitive and confidential programs.
•  Roboteam Portfolio: Actively used by DoD, IDF, NATO, US Marines, and leading militaries/governments in 30+ countries.
•  Roboteam Launch: ROBOX — next-gen robotics platform for integrated air-ground unmanned combat systems (rapid, containerized deployment).
•  New Partners: Anduril, Lockheed Martin, Dedrone.
•  DHS Grant: Submitted for World Cup security support.
•  Autonomous Border Project: Potential $100 million+ orders across phases (1–2 year timeline); not yet in backlog, with order expected soon.
•  Wåsp: Submitted to Drone Dominance Programme in January 2026.
•  System-of-Systems Expansion: Targeting new categories including maritime and directed energy (lasers/HPM).
•  M&A Pipeline: 20+ opportunities (growing); 7+ mature/profitable inbound from established players; pipeline value $500 million+ and expanding rapidly.

Ondas Capital

•  Specialized Event: Planned in the next 1–2 months.
•  Deployment Target: >$150 million (focused on TRL7+ technologies).
•  Investment Pipeline: 300+ opportunities; 9+ in active diligence.
•  Ecosystem Relationships: Expanding critical production/supply-chain partnerships in the US, EU, and UK.

Outlook

•  2024–2026 Phase: Service delivery and heavy investment stage — opex remains elevated.
•  2027+ Phase: Expected “expansion flywheel” with improved leverage and scaling.
•  Growth Drivers: Defense and HLS customers to lead order/revenue acceleration over the next 12–18 months.
•  Bookings Target: At least $300 million in the near term.
•  Gross Margins Target: 50%+ (aiming higher with volume/scale).
•  Opex Details: Targets to be shared in Q4 2025 earnings report.
•  Path to Profitability:
    •  EBITDA positive for product companies in Q3 2026.
    •  OAS in Q3 2027.
    •  Ondas Inc. overall in Q1 2028.
•  Positioning: Establishing as one of the market leaders now.
•  2030 Vision: $1.5 billion revenue target with 30% EBITDA margins — high-growth, scaled leader; $15 billion market cap viewed as very possible. Management is “all in.”

Additional Comments

•  Competitors are at the start of the path that Ondas has already completed (nearly a decade of development and execution).
•  Operating model follows “neoprimes” (scaled, integrated defense/tech platforms).
•  Unlikely for new players to emerge quickly due to long timelines, barriers to entry, and Ondas’ head start.
•  Current manufacturing capacity sufficient to meet revenue targets.
•  Border contract: Not yet reflected in backlog; order anticipated soon.
•  Merch store: Coming soon.

*Link: https://afterhour.com/Drone_Daddy/bfqV/dollaronds-investors-day-highl*

Take a look at the attached screenshots. 

I know I like to post a lot of hype content when my plays are in the green - playing off the dopamine from a winning trade. It’s fun to post huge gains and not fun to do the latter. But it’s important to show both sides of the coin. Anyone who doesn’t and sells you snake oil promises of growth only IS LYING TO YOU. 

Dont get me wrong - we had insane, realized profit in 2025 and 2026 but small cap wins are made in the trenches through long hours of research and unprecedented conviction. Not many investors have the stomach to open a trade and see their hard earned money down 30% in a couple weeks. Normal investors also don’t 300% their accounts in a matter of months and are happy with their yearly 10%. That’s a fine strategy and I respect it but it’s not me. 

I’m here to win. I trust in my work and when the deck is stacked against me I double down on my conviction because I know I’m right.

### Post #720: 2026-04-10 16:36 — `$NQ` `[Dd]`

**So You Want To Learn Futures Trading $NQ**

Dropping the full guide for my strat this weekend that has gotten me multiple payouts from prop firms which are built to make you lose. 7% achieve a singular payout with it being around 2% for multiple. 

I will be brutally honest with you. No guide alone will make you profitable. You have to be willing to put in the time on the charts and FOLLOW A SET OF RULES to be consistent. At the start I didn’t - I’m very competitive and couldn’t stand taking a loss so I’d revenge trade and lose even more money. Once I fixed my psychology and realized losing is a part of the game I started winning holistically. It will take time to surpress the emotions associated with drawdowns and profits - give it time. Trading psychology will help your conviction as an investor as well. 

I plan to give you the introduction and framework for what worked for me to give you a head start so you don’t have to spend months learning and creating a working strat. 

If you were ever curious about futures trading or didn’t know where to start - this is for you. 

Happy Friday 💎

### Post #721: 2026-04-10 21:33 — `$ONDS` `[Dd]`

**$ONDS Potential Dilution: My plan 💎**

Vote to increase the shares available from 800 million to 1.2 billion. This does NOT mean there is instant dilution. It gives them the option to distribute additional shares. Likely for acquisitions and other capital expenditures. Is it early? Yes. 

I see a lot of uproar but no one considering the obvious. If they dilute with a public offering do you really think it’s going to be at $9? No. They’ll do it at or near fresh ATHs. I’d suspect somewhere between the $20 and $28 dollar warrants. 

I bet we get a shock from this vote followed by additional drawdown into yearly volume lows. Short pressure will clear, we will hit new ATHs, an offering will take place and then I sell - similar to the last 2 offerings that saw pre run ups into 40-50% drawdown. I may even buy PUTs. It’s not that time yet though. 

We see $20 this year then maybe back to $12. Play both sides. Hope this clears things up for ya.

### Post #733: 2026-04-17 16:54 — `$ACHR, $LPTH, $ONDS, $UMAC, $RR` `[Dd]`

**Gem Vault Update + Sunday Livestream with @Bobdog @ 7:30pm CST**

Here’s an example of what I put out daily on Substack. Throw some likes and engagement on this so we can get as many people to the Sunday Livestream and I’ll post a few chapters of the Futures Guide on AH!

💎 Gem Vault Daily Update - April 17, 2026
Good Morning Everyone! Macro is doing a lot of the heavy lifting today - here's why. Iran's Foreign Minister officially declared the Strait of Hormuz completely open for all commercial vessels this morning, and the market responded how you’d think. Oil tumbled 10% while Wall Street rallied to another record high signaling some relief from geopolitical pressure that has been hanging over global markets since late February. At its worst, commercial traffic through the Strait of Hormuz had dropped more than 90%, disrupting roughly 25% of the world's seaborne oil trade making this extremely bullish for markets in the short term. SP500 Futures are up 1.26% and Nasdaq Futures are running +1.34% on the session. Today is also a major futures expiration day - contracts roll and volatility spikes. Expect elevated price action. 

On to the Gem Vault: 3 of 6 are green today. $ACHR at +3.29%, $RR building at +3.77%, and $ONDS holding steady at +1.57%. $UMAC is the sore spot at -6.41% on the daily but we did get some bullish news today: Unusual Machines appointed Chadd Cole as VP of Financial Planning & Analysis, reporting directly to CFO Brian Hoff, to build out the company's forecasting and operational planning capabilities as it scales production which is great to see for the growth story. The LEAPS are still up 12% and I'm  actively monitoring. $LPTH is down -2.08% on the stock, but the LEAPS are sitting at +71.43% and I'm targeting 100%.

Here's the thing I want you to sit with today: the Fear & Greed Index has flipped from Fear to near Extreme Greed in just one week. I'm bullish - the macro backdrop is the strongest it's been in months with Hormuz open, futures expiring, and our names largely participating. However, when greed shows up this fast, the disciplined move is to set trailing stops and have a profit-taking plan ready. Don't let a great setup turn into a loss. Ride the wave, but know where the exit is. Of course, I’ll keep you in the loop on my positions.

Stay patient. Stay the course. 💎

Sunday Livestream — 7:30 PM CST Options Selling Expert and AfterHour legend @Bobdog  should be joining us this week. You do NOT want to miss this one!

### Post #877: 2026-06-11 17:34 `[Dd]`

**A Few Tips For Futures Traders**

Here’s a few things that helped me in becoming profitable futures trading on props. 

1. Pick a system and stick to it. 
2. You should have your parameters in a trade set before you take it. Do not adjust your TP and Stop. I have never become a better trader during a trade. Close the window entirely
3. Set your entry where you would set your stop. This has been a little trick that has helped me alot recently when price runs past my key level. Ive hit some surgical entries from patience. 
4. Go for base hits, not home runs. Leave a runner if you really want to. 

Hope this helps someone. 💎

*Link: https://afterhour.com/Drone_Daddy/bqOn/a-few-tips-for-futures-traders*
